- 2026-07-19: **Fixed "Brand Picks" (and Video Close-Ups) rail not sliding for desktop mouse users on the buyer home feed.** Diagnosed empirically (Playwright) before touching anything: the rail genuinely had overflow content and a working `scrollLeft`, but a real mouse click-and-drag left it at `0`, and a plain vertical wheel doesn't pan a horizontal-only rail either — with `scrollbar-hide` removing the only other affordance, a desktop mouse user had no way to move it at all.
  - **New `src/hooks/useDragScroll.ts`** — click-and-drag horizontal scrolling for any `overflow-x-auto` rail: tracks pointer delta and sets `scrollLeft` directly, suspends `scroll-snap-type` for the duration of a drag (it was fighting a live JS-driven scroll and silently zeroing it out on the one rail using `snap-x snap-mandatory`), and swallows the click that follows a drag via `onClickCapture` so a dragged-over `Link`/button doesn't accidentally navigate/fire.
  - Wired into `NewArrivals.tsx`'s three horizontal rails: Brand Picks (the reported one), Video Close-Ups, and the "WHAT'S ON YOUR MIND" categories slider. Verified with real Playwright mouse-drag simulation (not just programmatic `scrollLeft`) that all three now move, and that a genuine (non-drag) click on a Call Now button or a card still behaves exactly as before (no accidental navigation, no broken clicks).

- 2026-07-19: **Buyer-home UI polish pass #2 (desktop) — categories centering, button text-align bug, sparse rails filled.** Follow-up to the polish pass below; same verification method (build + Playwright screenshots at 1280px/390px, mobile diffed for regressions).
  - **"WHAT'S ON YOUR MIND" categories now center when they fit the row** — `NewArrivals.tsx`: split the single scroll div into an outer `overflow-x-auto` viewport + an inner grid with `lg:w-max lg:mx-auto` (fixed `lg:auto-cols-[128px]` instead of a percentage, since percentage tracks can't resolve against an intrinsically-sized `w-max` box). Auto margins collapse to 0 if content ever exceeds the container, so scrolling still falls back correctly — deliberately avoided the classic `flex justify-center` + `overflow-x-auto` scroll-position bug. Mobile keeps its original `auto-cols-[22%]` edge-aligned layout untouched (no `lg:` prefix touches it).
  - **Fixed off-center "Submit Requirement" button text** — the two-column desktop layout from the prior pass put `lg:text-left` on an ancestor, which the full-width button's text silently inherited (it had no alignment of its own). Added explicit `text-center` directly on the button (`SubmitRequirementCard.tsx`) so it's centered regardless of the surrounding column's alignment.
  - **"Looking for these?" and "Brand Picks" no longer look sparse on wide screens** — both only had 3 mock items; `LOOKING_FOR_THESE` and `BRAND_PICKS` extended to 6 (reusing images already present elsewhere in the file, no new external URLs introduced). "Looking for these?" (`grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6`) gates the extra 3 behind `hidden lg:block` per card so **mobile still renders exactly the original 3-item row**; desktop shows a full 6-across row. "Brand Picks" is a horizontal scroll rail, so the extra items simply extend what's reachable by scrolling — mobile's initially-visible cards are byte-for-byte unchanged, desktop's wider viewport now has enough cards to fill (with a slight peek of the next card, signaling scrollability rather than reading as empty).

- 2026-07-19: **Buyer-home + landing UI polish pass (desktop).** Four focused layout fixes, verified via production build + Playwright screenshots (desktop 1280px & mobile 390px).
  - **Landing logo shrunk** — `Landing.tsx` header `CosoraLogo` height 34→28 (mobile) / 40→32 (desktop). Footer logo untouched.
  - **"Call Now" CTAs now bottom-align across a row** — cards had ragged button positions when text wrapped (e.g. a two-line manufacturer/price). Made the card a `flex flex-col h-full`, the content block `flex flex-col flex-1`, and inserted a `flex-1` spacer before the button so every CTA pins to the card's bottom edge regardless of text height. Applied to both `NewArrivals.tsx`'s local `ProductCard` **and** the shared `components/buyer/ListingProductCard.tsx` (fixes For You / Trends / Following too).
  - **"Today's New In" grid fills complete desktop rows** — a live-product count like 6 left a half-empty row on `lg:grid-cols-4`. Added `desktopCols = cols*2`; a `displayProducts` useMemo pads the feed up to the next multiple of `desktopCols` by cycling existing products (unique `-fillN` ids for keys), and `productsPerChunk` is now `desktopCols*3` so every chunk (incl. the last, before each Submit-Requirement box) is edge-to-edge on desktop. `desktopCols` is a multiple of the mobile column count, so mobile stays full too.
  - **"Looking for products?" card optimised for desktop** — was `lg:max-w-md`, stuck to the left. Wrapper → `lg:max-w-4xl lg:mx-auto` (centered, wider); `SubmitRequirementCard.tsx` now renders a 2-column desktop layout **only** for the `onQuickRfq` variant (intro/CTA left, quick-action rows right with a left divider `lg:border-l`). Plain variant (ForYou bottom bar, SearchResults empty state) and all mobile layouts unchanged.

- 2026-07-19: **Closed the real `ad_location_scope` gap — enforcement in the paid ad-publish path (edge functions), not just the direct-insert trigger.** The prior `enforce_ad_location_scope` trigger only guarded direct authenticated inserts; the actual campaign publish runs as the service role in `razorpay-verify-payment` / `razorpay-webhook`, which the trigger's `current_user` guard bypasses — so the money-changing-hands path still trusted the client's `target_cities`. Now both functions resolve the vendor's **effective plan server-side** before inserting (read `vendor_subscriptions` + `subscription_plans` directly, since `get_vendor_plan` is self/admin-guarded and returns null for a service-role call on another vendor — same effective-plan rule: active + `current_period_end > now()`, else Free).
  - **Free (`ad_location_scope='none'`) → not published + flagged.** A payment that reaches publish for a Free vendor doesn't create an ad; the order is marked `ad_orders.status='refund_review'` (durable trace for manual refund/admin review — never a silent drop). Migration `20260719122000_ad_orders_refund_review_status.sql` extends the `ad_orders_status_check` lifecycle (`created/paid/failed`) with `refund_review` (kept distinct from `failed`, which is a gateway/publish failure owing no refund). Live/webhook flip the claimed order; demo mode (no intent row) records a `demo_blocked_<uuid>` trace row. Trace helpers now check the write succeeded so the response never claims a `refundFlagged` that didn't persist.
  - **Paid tier over its city allowance → clamped, still published.** `target_cities` is trimmed to the first N (`state_1`→1, `state_4`→4, `pan_india`/`global`→unlimited) and the ad publishes normally. Deliberate: ad pricing doesn't vary by city count, so clamping removes no paid-for line item; failing the whole order over a targeting detail would deny a vendor who paid. (`verify-payment` v5, `verify_jwt=true`; `webhook` v3, `verify_jwt=false`; both redeployed. Client `Advertisements.tsx` city UI, ad pricing, and the three prior triggers untouched.)
  - **Verified against the real deployed function** (signed in as `demo-vendor@cosora.dev`, plan temporarily flipped then restored to its exact Gold snapshot; all test rows cleaned up): Basic vendor, spec with 3 target cities → response `requested:3, allowed:1` and the **published** ad row has `target_cities:["mumbai"]` (clamped, not the requested 3). Effective-Free vendor → response `plan_not_eligible / refundFlagged:true`, **0 ads published**, and an `ad_orders` row `status='refund_review'` (amount ₹154, original 2-city spec captured). Advisors unaffected (CHECK-constraint change only, no new definer functions).

- 2026-07-19: **Server-side enforcement for the 3 remaining bypassable tier flags (`has_auto_catalog`, `ad_location_scope`, `leads_per_month`).** Follow-up to the `product_cap` fix; migration `20260719121000_enforce_plan_limits_catalogues_ads_quotes.sql`, applied live to `vxdhhgdfubqedfpwfyrb`. Each new trigger fn mirrors `enforce_product_cap()` exactly: SECURITY INVOKER + `current_user <> 'authenticated'` service-role/seed bypass, effective-plan resolution via `vendor_subscriptions` (active + `current_period_end > now()`, else `free`), `P0001` messages. Proven with impersonated `authenticated` inserts (all rolled back); `enforce_product_cap` left untouched; advisors clean (all three are SECURITY INVOKER — not in the definer lists).
  - **`has_auto_catalog` → `enforce_catalogue_plan()` / `trg_catalogues_plan` on `catalogues`.** Blocks a vendor whose effective plan lacks the feature (Free) from creating a catalogue (any INSERT) or transitioning a draft into a visible state; Basic+ unaffected. `createCatalogue` runs as the authenticated client, so this fully gates the real flow. **Tests:** Free INSERT → blocked (`P0001`); Basic (in-txn sub) INSERT → allowed; Gold INSERT → allowed; service-role INSERT → allowed (bypass).
  - **`ad_location_scope` → `enforce_ad_location_scope()` / `trg_ads_location_scope` on `advertisements`.** (a) Free (`scope none`) blocked from creating any ad; (b) paid tiers capped at `jsonb_array_length(target_cities)` ≤ allowance (`state_1`→1, `state_4`→4, `pan_india`/`global`→unlimited). UPDATE blocks only a location-count INCREASE beyond allowance, so same-scope edits (pause/budget/reducing cities) are never collaterally blocked. **Tests:** Free INSERT → blocked; Basic 2-location INSERT → blocked, exactly-1 → allowed; existing 2-city ad budget edit → allowed; increasing 2→3 cities → blocked; Gold 5-city → allowed. **Caveat (documented, matches the pattern):** the primary *active-campaign* publish path is the Razorpay verify/webhook edge function inserting via the service role, which — like seeds — the `current_user` guard intentionally bypasses; this trigger hardens the direct authenticated insert/update surface RLS exposes (any non-`active` ad). No buyer-geo delivery was built (still "rolling out").
  - **`leads_per_month` → `enforce_lead_cap()` / `trg_quotes_lead_cap` on `quotes`.** Blocks a new quote when the vendor's distinct-RFQ count for the current period ≥ `leads_per_month` (live count matching `get_vendor_plan.leads_used`; window = active sub's `current_period_start`, else calendar month). Re-quoting an already-quoted RFQ (the `submitQuote` upsert → ON CONFLICT UPDATE, detected via existing `(rfq_id,vendor_id)` row) and any UPDATE are exempt — editing never consumes a new lead. `subscription_usage` deliberately left unwired (live-count only). `submitQuote` runs authenticated, so this fully gates the real flow. **Tests** (Free cap 10, 11 synthetic RFQs in-txn): 10 distinct quotes → allowed (`leads_used`=10); 11th → blocked (`P0001`); at cap, upsert re-quote of an existing RFQ → allowed; at cap, plain UPDATE of an existing quote → allowed; service-role bypass confirmed.

- 2026-07-19: **Subscription tier-features audit (senior QA) + fixed the confirmed `product_cap` server-side bypass.** Went flag-by-flag through `subscription_plans.limits` (all 10 correctly seeded per tier) and classified each: (a) genuinely enforced, (b) exists-but-bypassable (client-only, no DB backing), (c) not built. Verified empirically against live data + impersonated `authenticated` sessions (all rolled back), not by reading code alone.
  - **FIX — `product_cap` now enforced server-side (migration `20260719120000_enforce_product_cap.sql`).** New `enforce_product_cap()` + `trg_products_cap` BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE trigger on `products` (SECURITY INVOKER, `current_user='authenticated'` service-role bypass like `enforce_products_moderation`). Counts the vendor's listed products (`under_review`+`live` — same set `get_vendor_plan` reports) against their **effective** plan's cap (mirrors the expiry rule; `-1`=unlimited). Also blocks the draft→`under_review` **laundering** path (UPDATE into the counted set), closing the edit-mode hole the client gate left open. Existing over-cap seed vendors are untouched (forward-looking, non-destructive). **Proven:** pre-fix, an impersonated Tirupur (Free, cap 2, 5 listed) INSERT past cap **succeeded** (5→6); post-fix it's blocked (`P0001`), exact boundary at cap 2 (2 ok, 3rd blocked), drafts still allowed, laundering blocked, Gold under-cap allowed, edits of existing live rows allowed, service-role/seed inserts unaffected. Security advisors: no new findings (my fn is SECURITY INVOKER). The client gate in `Upload.tsx` stays as UX; the DB is now the source of truth.
  - **Audit results (unchanged this pass unless noted):** `has_verified_badge` **(a)** — OR-logic intact (`trustSealFromParts` in products/rfqs/vendor mappers + `get_vendor_plan.trust_seal`). `search_boost_tier` **(a, client-side)** — real sort weight in `products.ts` (`fetchLiveProducts`/`fetchCatalogue`/`sortTrending` order by boost desc); confirmed live that Gold vendor's products rank above all boost-0 vendors. Plan badge on the seller sidebar **(a)** — real `useVendorPlan().plan.name` chip + live usage line. Category-matched premium leads **(a, client-side)** — `fetchOpenRfqs` flags+sorts RFQs matching a paid vendor's product categories. `leads_per_month` **(b)** — client-blocked in `OpenRfqLeads` off a live-computed `leads_used`, but no trigger on `quotes`; **`subscription_usage` table has 0 rows / is never written by anything**. `ad_location_scope` **(b)** — client-only city-target cap + Free-can't-advertise gate; no server validation (buyer geo-delivery honestly labeled "rolling out"). `has_auto_catalog` **(b)** — client blocks Free in `UploadCatalogue.tsx`, but **proven bypassable**: an impersonated Free vendor inserted a `live` catalogue directly. `has_crm` **(c)** — only a `plan.display` comparison-table row; no CRM surface exists. `has_international` **(c as a gate)** — no buyer-geography/visibility gating anywhere; exists only as an honest "International-ready" display chip. Lead access channel differentiation (Free=website … Gold/VIP=SMS) **(c)** — never built; only a comparison-table string, all tiers share the same RFQ pool. `has_dedicated_am` / `has_realtime_alerts` **(c, intentionally deferred)** — scaffold columns only (`account_manager_id`, `real_time_alerts_enabled`), shown as comparison rows; nothing fakes an assigned human or alert delivery ("Set Alerts" just links to `/leads`). No new work on AM/alerts/international/CRM/leads/ads per the task's explicit boundaries.

- 2026-07-18: **Buyer product-listing production pass — real stats, real recommendations, persisted preferences, hardened writes.**
  - **Duplicate `/browse` retired:** `App.tsx` `/browse` now `<Navigate to="/search" replace />` (removed the `BrowseProducts` import/route + fake data); the in-app `/browse` links in `Landing.tsx`, `Index.tsx`, `Register.tsx` repointed to `/search`, and `DashboardHeader.tsx` "New Arrivals" → `/home/new-arrivals`.
  - **Product stats made real (migration `product_view_enquiry_counters`):** SECURITY DEFINER RPCs `increment_product_view(p)` / `increment_product_enquiry(p)` (mirror the ad_impression/ad_click pattern, `where … status='live'`, granted anon+authenticated). `products.ts` gained `recordProductView`/`recordProductEnquiry`; `ProductDetail.tsx` bumps **views_count** once per session (sessionStorage-deduped, on open of a live product — not localStorage-only recordView) and **enquiries_count** on the Chat/message-vendor action. **sold_count left unwired and reported plainly** — no product-level "deal completed" event exists (quotes/rfqs carry no product_id, no orders table).
  - **Literal "Product name" fixed** on buyer cards in `NewArrivals.tsx` (×2) and `SearchResults.tsx` → the real `{product.name}`/`{item.name}`/`{p.name}`.

A nonexistent id and an RLS-blocked (non-live, not-owned) id both resolve to `null` via `maybeSingle`, so both render the exact same not-found page — no existence oracle.
  - **Write hardening:** new `assertWrote(rows, action)` guard in `products.ts`; `deleteProduct`/`updateProduct` now `.select("id")` and throw on a silent zero-row write (RLS no-op no longer looks like success).
  - **Recommendations are real (rule-based, no AI):** buyer preferences now persist account-tied to a new **`buyer_profiles.preferred_categories text[]`** (migration `buyer_preferred_categories`). New `src/lib/buyerCategories.ts` holds the shared buyer categories + `PREF_TO_DB_CATEGORY_NAMES` mapping; `preferencesStore.ts` reads/writes the DB column (`dbSaveCategories`/`setPreferencesUser`, hydrated on sign-in via `StoreSync`, localStorage only when signed out); `ForYou.tsx` and `InterestPreference.tsx` share the same source. **"You might also like"** = products in the buyer's preferred categories ranked `enquiries_count` desc, `views_count` tiebreak (fallback to the current product's category), `useYouMightLike`. **"Brand Picks"** = the same vendor's other live products, `useVendorOtherProducts`. Both `live`-only, exclude the current product, and **hide when empty** (no padding). Removed the mock `brandPicks`/`youMightLike` fixtures.
  - **InterestPreference edit path fixed:** the shared screen now branches Save + back by route — `/auth/interest-preference` → `/auth/terms` (registration), `/profile/interest-preference` → back to `/profile` (edit); `Profile.tsx` Interest & Preferences "Edit" now routes to `/profile/interest-preference` (real entry point). Verified there is **no cold-load hydration race** in the pre-fill (the `useEffect([prefs.categories])` re-syncs reactively and `set()` always yields a fresh categories reference) — reported as already-correct, no change made.
  - `database.types.ts` updated (new RPCs + `preferred_categories`). `npx tsc --noEmit --skipLibCheck` clean after each task.

- 2026-07-17: **Admin RBAC (follow-up) — role-gated the remaining whole-row admin write policies.** Migration `20260717130100_role_gate_products_ads_vendorprofiles_writes.sql`. Three tables still had plain `is_admin()`-gated writes; brought them into line with the subscription-tables pattern by role-checking only the admin branch of each OR (owner conditions preserved verbatim): `products` insert/update/delete → `product_moderator`, `advertisements` insert/update/delete → `ads_moderator` (INSERT keeps its `status<>'active'` owner guard), `vendor_profiles` `vprofiles_write` (ALL) → `vendor_ops`. Reads untouched. Verified live (rolled back) with a `support`-role admin acting on rows owned by other vendors: INSERT → RLS violation, UPDATE/DELETE → 0 rows; the matching role → 1 row (proving it's the gate, not a missing row). This layers on top of the column-level triggers from the first migration.

- 2026-07-17: **Admin RBAC — fine-grained admin roles + real role-gated write enforcement (DB layer for the admin panel).** Migration `20260717130000_admin_roles_and_role_gated_rls.sql`, applied live to `vxdhhgdfubqedfpwfyrb`. This is Prompt 1 of 2; the admin panel is a separate repo that's only UI on top of this. `is_admin()`/`profiles.is_admin` are unchanged ("is this an admin at all"); this adds a second layer — *which* admin role.
  - **Schema:** enum `admin_role_type` (super_admin/product_moderator/vendor_ops/ads_moderator/finance_admin/support), nullable `profiles.admin_role` (null = admin with no role yet → fails closed), `admin_role()` (SECURITY DEFINER, mirrors `is_admin()`), and `vendor_profiles.account_status` (`active`/`suspended`, for the panel's vendor-suspension feature).
  - **Enforcement in 3 places (reads stay plain `is_admin()` — role-gating is writes only):** (1) **RLS** — `vendor_subscriptions`/`subscription_invoices` admin writes require `finance_admin` (added a `subscription_invoices` admin write policy that didn't exist). (2) **`BEFORE` triggers** (SECURITY INVOKER; RLS can't see *which column* changed) — products.status→live/rejected needs `product_moderator` (owner keeps draft↔under_review), vendor_profiles.is_verified/account_status needs `vendor_ops`, ad status changes on an ad you don't own need `ads_moderator` (vendors keep pause/resume of their own), and the **keystone**: only `super_admin` may change `is_admin`/`admin_role` (closes the pre-existing self-promotion hole). Triggers bypass for service-role/SECURITY DEFINER contexts via a `current_user='authenticated'` guard, so edge functions and the approve RPC are unaffected. (3) **`approve_vendor_content` RPC** (SECURITY DEFINER → bypasses RLS) tightened from "any admin" to `product_moderator`.
  - **Bootstrap:** seed admin `demo-admin@cosora.dev` promoted to `super_admin` (else role-gating deadlocks). Further super_admins are granted out-of-band (service role / SQL).
  - **Verified live** (impersonating `authenticated` with JWT claims, all rolled back): non-admin self-promote → blocked; vendor self-suspend → blocked; `support`-role admin doing a `vendor_ops` action → blocked; `super_admin` granting a role → succeeds. Security advisor clean apart from the same SECURITY-DEFINER-executable note the pre-existing `is_admin()` already carries.

- 2026-07-17: **Advertise feature — fixed the fake/decorative parts (targeting, trust-seal ads, competitor intel, old-ads, pricing catalog).** The real Razorpay create→verify→webhook flow, `advertisements` writes, My-Ads list, and buyer-side `active_ads`/SponsoredRail were kept as-is.
  - **Targeting is now real (was 100% decorative).** New `advertisements.target_categories` / `target_cities` (jsonb) threaded end-to-end: `buildSpec` → `AdSpec` → `adRows()` in razorpay-verify-payment + webhook → the row (migration `ad_targeting`). **Category** targeting genuinely filters buyer-side: `active_ads(max, filter_category)` returns untargeted ads (run everywhere) + ads targeting that category; wired on ProductDetail via the product's real `category_id` (new category-filtered SponsoredRail). Category picker now uses the vendor's **real DB categories** (`useVendorCategories`). **Gender** targeting was removed (no buyer gender signal, no plan tie — decorative). **City** targeting is plan-scope-limited (Part 5) + persisted, but honestly labelled "geo-based delivery rolling out" (no buyer location signal exists to filter on).
  - **Goals do something real:** selecting a campaign goal pre-selects the placement types that serve it (`GOAL_PLACEMENTS`), instead of being an inert picker.
  - **Trust-seal ad types now grant something:** buying `trustedSeal`/`verifiedCertificate` writes a time-bound `vendor_ad_verifications` row (expires with the campaign) + denormalised `vendor_profiles.ad_verified_until`, granted server-side in the same publish path (`grantSeals` in verify-payment + webhook, migration `ad_verifications`). Buyer trust seal is now `is_verified OR active plan OR active ad-verification` (extended `trustSealFromParts`, threaded through products/vendor/rfq mappers). The buy flow shows "Included in your {plan}" and blocks re-purchase when the vendor's plan already grants the seal (no double-charge).
  - **Pricing catalogs reconciled:** deleted the invented `ADVERTISE_CARDS` (different names/prices than the real catalog); the "Popular ad types" strip is now generated from real `AD_TYPES`/`AD_PRICE` — every displayed price is actually purchasable.
  - **CompetitorAds** (was fully fabricated named competitors) → real anonymized **category benchmarks** from `ad_category_benchmarks` RPC (per-category product/vendor counts, avg price/views, your-vs-peer reviews & photos) computed from actual data, with a "no individual competitor identified" disclaimer.
  - **OldAdvertisements** (was mock) → the vendor's real campaign history via `useMyAds` (effective status: active campaigns past `ends_at` read as Ended), with real Views/Clicks/CTR/budget and working Pause/Resume (`updateAdStatus`).
  - Verified live: create-order→`not_configured`→demo publish persists targeting + grants the seal; `active_ads` category filter confirmed (targeted ad shows only in its category, untargeted always). Live signature path shares `publishOrder`/`adRows`/`grantSeals` with the tested demo path. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean.

Now `subscription-verify-payment`'s shared `activateSubscription` **refuses any invite-only plan** server-side (redeployed v2). Verified: demo-activate VIP → `{ok:false,error:"invite_only"}`.

Genuine sort input, not simulated.

- 2026-07-17: **Vendor subscription system — 5 tiers, real Razorpay billing, and limits enforced at the point of action.** Greenfield: first tracked SQL migrations in the repo (`supabase/migrations/`), applied live to project `vxdhhgdfubqedfpwfyrb`.
  - **Schema (migrations `subscription_tables` → `subscription_hardening`):** `subscription_plans` (text-slug PK free/basic/silver/gold/vip; prices, `limits` jsonb + `display` jsonb — every spec row modelled as data, no scattered booleans), `vendor_subscriptions` (one row/vendor, `status`/`billing_cycle`/`current_period_*`/`auto_renew` reminder-only + scaffold `account_manager_id`/`real_time_alerts_enabled`), `subscription_usage`, `subscription_invoices` (amount/gst/gstin/tds/invoice_number/period/pdf_url), `subscription_payment_orders` (mirrors `ad_orders`: created→paid, idempotent). Added denormalised `vendor_profiles.plan_id` + `plan_expires_at` cache. RLS: owner/admin **read** only; all writes via edge functions (service role). Seeded 5 tiers (yearly = 10× monthly ≈ 17% off; trust seal on **every paid tier** per override; no matchmaking-AI field) + demo-vendor on active Gold.
  - **RPC `get_vendor_plan(v)`** (SECURITY DEFINER, self/admin-guarded): resolves the **effective** plan (falls back to Free on read when a period lapses — the expiry rule), with limits + **live** usage (products_used = under_review+live count; leads_used = distinct RFQs quoted this period). Single read model for `useVendorPlan`. Also `next_invoice_number()` + `expire_subscriptions()` sweeper (service-role only).
  - **Edge functions (deployed):** `subscription-create-order` / `subscription-verify-payment` / `subscription-webhook` — modelled on the ad functions. Amount computed server-side from the plan (+18% GST); demo/simulated activation when `RAZORPAY_*` unset (verified: `not_configured` → demo activate → plan flips → invoice `INV-2026-000001` with GST ₹126 on ₹699). Verify/webhook activate idempotently via the created→paid claim.
  - **Client:** `src/lib/plan.ts` (pure model/helpers: limits, `trustSealFromParts`, tier styles, ad-scope), `src/lib/queries/subscriptions.ts` (`useSubscriptionPlans`/`useVendorPlan`/`useVendorInvoices` + `purchaseSubscription` orchestration). Rebuilt `Subscription.tsx` on live data (5 tiers, live usage bars, data-driven comparison table, GSTIN/PAN persisted to profile+invoice, live billing history) + new printable `InvoiceDetail` page (`/subscription/invoice/:id`, browser print-to-PDF).
  - **Enforcement at point of action:** `Upload.tsx` blocks publishing beyond `product_cap` (drafts/edits allowed); `OpenRfqLeads` blocks quoting past the monthly lead cap + shows remaining; `Advertisements.tsx` gates Free from ads and caps location targets to the tier's `ad_location_scope`. **Trust seal** now derived everywhere buyers see it (`is_verified OR active paid plan`) via the query mappers — never overwrites the admin `is_verified` column. **Category-matched premium leads** (rule-based): paid vendors get RFQs matching their product categories flagged + sorted first. Sidebar shows the vendor's plan chip + usage.
  - **Config still needed for live payments:** `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID`, `RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET`, `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (Supabase → Edge Functions → Secrets; webhook → `/functions/v1/subscription-webhook`, event `payment.captured`). Scaffolded-not-real: account-manager assignment, WhatsApp/SMS alerts, sales concierge, search-ranking boost (stored as `search_boost_tier`, not yet wired into buyer ranking). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean.

- 2026-07-05: **Razorpay hardening — webhook backstop, idempotent publish, and ad-creation RLS lockdown.** Follow-up making the ad payments production-safe.
  - **Payment intents (migration `ad_payment_intents_and_rls_lockdown`):** new `ad_orders` table (`order_id` pk, `vendor_id`, `spec` jsonb, `amount`, `status` created→paid, RLS: owner-read only, no client writes). `razorpay-create-order` now records the full spec + amount here (server-side) and returns just `{orderId, amount, keyId}`. `razorpay-verify-payment` (live) verifies the signature then **publishes by claiming the intent** (`UPDATE … status='created'→'paid' RETURNING`, then inserts the ads) — so it and the webhook are **idempotent** and never double-publish; spec/vendor come from the stored intent, not the client.
  - **Webhook (`razorpay-webhook`, `verify_jwt=false`):** server-to-server backstop — verifies the `x-razorpay-signature` HMAC of the raw body (`RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET`) and publishes the same intent on `payment.captured`/`order.paid`, so a closed browser mid-redirect still gets the campaigns. Deployed + reachable (`not_configured` until the secret is set).
  - **RLS lockdown:** replaced the `advertisements_write` ALL policy with insert/update/delete policies — clients **cannot insert an `active` ad** (`status <> 'active'` check) and a `guard_ad_activation` BEFORE-UPDATE trigger blocks laundering a draft into active (activation only from active/paused, or service role/admin). So the only path to an active ad is a server-verified payment. Verified in SQL: active-insert blocked, draft→active blocked, pause/resume still works.
  - **Demo path now server-side too:** the no-keys fallback calls `razorpay-verify-payment` with `{demo:true, spec}` (new `publishDemoAds` in `payments.ts`) — the edge function creates the ads, so the client never inserts ads directly even in demo. Removed the client `createAd` path from the flow.
  - **Extra secret for the webhook:** `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (in addition to `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID`/`RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET`); set the same value as the webhook's signing secret in the Razorpay dashboard, pointing it at `/functions/v1/razorpay-webhook`. Types updated (`ad_orders`), `npm run build` clean.

- 2026-07-05: **Real Razorpay gateway wired for ad purchases (server-verified), with a graceful fallback until keys are set.** Replaces the mock-only checkout with a production payment flow.
  - **Edge functions (deployed, `verify_jwt` on):** `supabase/functions/razorpay-create-order` computes the amount **server-side** from the ad spec (mirrors AD_TYPES pricing — never trusts a client amount), creates a Razorpay order via the Orders API (Basic auth with `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID:RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET`), and returns `{orderId, amount, keyId}`. `razorpay-verify-payment` recomputes the HMAC-SHA256 signature (`orderId|paymentId`), and only on a match **creates the campaigns server-side** via the service role — one active `advertisements` row per promoted product — with the **vendor id taken from the JWT** (not the client body). Both return `{error:"not_configured"}` until the secrets exist.
  - **Client (`src/lib/queries/payments.ts`):** `createRazorpayOrder` / `openRazorpayCheckout` (dynamically loads `checkout.js`, coral theme, resolves on success / rejects `"dismissed"`) / `verifyRazorpayPayment`. `Advertisements.tsx` "Pay & Publish" now: create order → if configured, open **real Razorpay Checkout** → verify → ads publish (server-side); if **not configured**, opens the existing simulated modal (now labelled "Demo checkout — no real charge") and publishes client-side. Verified deployed + reachable: both functions return `not_configured` with the anon JWT, so the fallback path is active today.
  - **To go live:** set `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID` + `RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET` in Supabase → Edge Functions → Secrets. No redeploy/code change needed — the flow switches to real Razorpay automatically. (Optional hardening later: tighten `advertisements` INSERT RLS so only the verify function can create `active` ads.)

- 2026-07-05: **Functional advertisements — select type → pay → publish to the buyer feed → track analytics.** The ad builder created a bare row and nothing reached buyers; now it's an end-to-end loop.
  - **Schema (migration `advertisements_buyer_publish_and_analytics`):** buyers read active ads through a SECURITY DEFINER RPC **`active_ads(max_count)`** that joins `advertisements → products (live) → vendor_profiles` and returns only safe fields (product name/image/price/vendor — never budgets). Impressions/clicks bump via **`ad_impression(ad)`** / **`ad_click(ad)`** RPCs (all three intentionally granted to `anon`+`authenticated` so buyers — signed in or not — can view/track). `createAd` gained `endsAt`.
  - **Vendor create flow (`Advertisements.tsx`):** Step 2 now selects the vendor's **real live products** (`useMyProducts`, image tiles, stores product UUIDs) instead of placeholder icons. "Create Your Ad" became **Pay & Publish** → a **PaymentModal** (UPI/Card mock checkout, simulated gateway latency) that on success creates **one active campaign per selected product** (with `product_id`, product image, `daily_budget`, `placement`, `ends_at`). Empty-state links to `/upload` when the vendor has no products. (No real gateway — a Razorpay/Stripe integration would swap into PaymentModal + activate on webhook.)
  - **Buyer publishing:** new `src/components/buyer/SponsoredRail.tsx` (`useActiveAds`) renders a **"Sponsored"** rail of promoted product cards on the buyer home (`NewArrivals`, above Video Close-Ups). It logs an impression per ad on view and, on tap, logs a click and opens `/product/:id`.
  - **Analytics:** `queries/ads.ts` gained `useActiveAds` + `logAdImpression`/`logAdClick`. The vendor's **"Your Campaigns"** now shows a totals strip (Active · Impressions · Clicks · CTR), a per-row CTR, and each campaign's product thumbnail.

Public read; buyer-owned writes; one review per buyer per entity (upsert). No aggregate trigger (nothing to sync — the client computes avg/count, falling back to the mock rating until real reviews exist).

RLS identical shape (public SELECT; writes scoped to `buyer_id = auth.uid()`; admin delete). Trigger **`sync_product_rating`** keeps `products.rating_avg`/`reviews_count` in sync; reuses the hardened `touch_reviews_updated_at`. Verified anon read OK + anon insert RLS-blocked. Regenerated `database.types.ts`.

Falls back to the mock template for non-DB demo products. Empty state reworded.

- 2026-07-05: **Reviews connected end-to-end (buyer ⇄ vendor), fully DB-backed.** Buyer reviews used to just `toast` and both review pages were mock; now a buyer's review reaches the vendor and drives the vendor's real rating.
  - **Schema (migration `reviews_buyer_to_vendor` + `reviews_harden_functions`):** new **`reviews`** table (`vendor_id → vendor_profiles`, `buyer_id → auth.users`, `rating 1-5`, `body`, denormalized `reviewer_name`/`reviewer_company`, `reply_body`/`replied_at`, `created_at`/`updated_at`, **unique (vendor_id, buyer_id)** so editing is an upsert). RLS: **public SELECT** (buyers + anon browsers + the vendor all read), INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE scoped to `buyer_id = auth.uid()` (+ admin delete). A trigger **`sync_vendor_rating`** keeps `vendor_profiles.rating_avg`/`reviews_count` in sync on every insert/update/delete; a SECURITY DEFINER RPC **`reply_to_review`** lets a vendor add/edit only the reply fields on reviews about them (never the buyer's rating/body). Verified: anon read OK, anon insert RLS-blocked, aggregate trigger correct; hardened `search_path` + revoked RPC execute on the trigger fns (advisors clean apart from the pre-existing baseline). Regenerated `database.types.ts`.
  - **Query layer:** new `src/lib/queries/reviews.ts` — `useVendorReviews(vendorId)` (list + avg + count + 5→1 breakdown), `useMyReviews()` (buyer's own, joined to vendor identity), and `useReviewMutations()` → `submit` (upsert, auto-fills reviewer name/company from `buyer_profiles`/`profiles`), `updateReview`, `removeReview`, `reply` (RPC). All invalidate `["reviews"]` + `["vendor_profile"]`.
  - **Buyer VendorProfile (`/vendor/:id`):** Write-a-Review modal now really submits (create/edit, with saving state); the Reviews & Ratings block shows the **real** average, count, star breakdown, and a live review list (reviewer, stars, date, body, plus the vendor's reply) with a View-all toggle. Falls back to the `vendor_profiles` aggregate until a vendor has real reviews.
  - **Vendor Reviews page (`/reviews`):** reads the signed-in vendor's real reviews (rating, breakdown, count) and **Reply** now posts through the RPC and persists (was local-only). Mock array removed.
  - **Buyer My Reviews (`/profile/reviews`):** lists the buyer's real vendor reviews; Edit and Delete now hit the DB. Seed array removed. (Product/Service review types remain as future filters.)
  - **Seed:** added 9 realistic demo reviews across Demo Textiles Co. (4.4★ ×5) and Tirupur Textiles (4.8★ ×4), two with vendor replies, so the loop is visible immediately. `npm run build` clean.

Regenerated `database.types.ts`.

New `queries/vendorOnboarding.ts` — the 8-step `Onboarding.tsx` now **persists** on completion: identity/address/owner/PAN·GSTIN·CIN → `vendor_profiles` (+`onboarding_complete`, `profile_score`), KYC doc rows → `vendor_documents`, and the step-7 product → a real `under_review` listing.

  - Verified: `tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` clean; RLS enabled with policies on all 3 new tables; demo-vendor's 6 products already `live`. Moderation preserved — vendor uploads enter `under_review` and appear to buyers only after `approve_vendor_content`.

New `src/lib/callStore.ts` (`useSyncExternalStore`) + `src/components/buyer/CallNumberModal.tsx` mounted in `App.tsx`. `useCallVendor` now routes through `placeCall` (still logs the call + falls back to chat when no number).

Also wired: **ChatThread** header call button (was a toast → now dials the vendor), the two **NewArrivals** decorative "Call Now" tiles (Looking-for-these / Brand Picks, were dead), and the **Freelancers / FreelancerProfile / ServiceVendors / ServiceVendorProfile** Call buttons (were chat-only). Non-DB entities (freelancers/service vendors, decorative tiles) use a deterministic `demoPhone(seed)` so the button places a real call (show/dial) without a stored number. MyQuotes' quote Call already routed via `callVendor`.

- 2026-07-04: **Call Now → native dialer + logged in the Calls tab.** Every buyer "Call Now" used to just `navigate('/chats/${vendorId}')`; now it opens the phone dialer pre-filled with the vendor's number and records the call.
  - **Schema (`calls_and_vendor_phone`):** added `vendor_profiles.phone` (public-read; seeded `+91…` demo numbers for all 6 vendors) and a `calls` table (`buyer_id`, `vendor_id`, `direction` check outgoing/incoming/missed, `product_context`, `created_at`) with RLS — SELECT `buyer_id/vendor_id = auth.uid() or is_admin()`, INSERT/write `buyer_id = auth.uid()`, indexed on buyer. Regenerated `database.types.ts`.
  - **Hook `src/lib/queries/calls.ts`:** `useCallVendor()` — on click looks up the vendor's `phone`+`brand_name`, logs an outgoing `calls` row (signed-in only; invalidates `["calls",uid]`), then `window.location.href = tel:<digits>`. On desktop (where `tel:` may no-op) it also toasts the number. **No number on file → falls back to opening the chat thread** + toast. `useCalls()` returns the buyer's real calls grouped Today/Yesterday/<date> in the existing `CallRecord` shape.
  - **Wired ~13 buyer Call Now buttons** to `useCallVendor` (shared `ListingProductCard`/`BuyerProductCard`/`VideoCloseUpsViewer`; pages NewArrivals/Trends/Sale/ProductDetail/VendorProfile/SearchResults/RecentlyViewed/SavedCollectionDetail/BrowseProducts/MyQuotes) — leaving their **Chat** buttons opening chat. `Chat.tsx` Calls tab now reads real grouped calls when signed in (static fallback signed out) and the green call button dials.
  - **Limitation (by OS design):** `tel:` only *pre-fills* the dialer — a web app cannot auto-place a call. Real auto-dialing needs a telephony provider (Twilio/Exotel) — a separate integration.
  - Verified: RLS round-trip with a demo-buyer JWT (self-insert 201, spoof 403, anon read `[]`, public phone read OK); `tsc`/build clean; deployed.

- 2026-07-04: **Search Results filters — made item-focused.** The panel showed facets that weren't relevant to the searched item; rewrote `src/lib/searchFilters.ts` so filters are tight to the query.
  - **Removed the parent-category dump:** searching "t-shirt" no longer prepends a "Category" facet listing every menswear sub (Jeans, Jackets, Kurtas…). Instead each domain shows a focused **item Type** facet.
  - **Sub-item detection for tops & bottoms:** apparel-top resolves tee vs polo vs shirt vs women's-top; apparel-bottom resolves jeans vs shorts vs joggers vs trousers — each with a tailored Type + only the attributes that matter (e.g. jeans → Jeans Style / Wash / Waist / Length; shirt → Shirt Type / Collar / Sleeve). Fixed the "t-shirt contains the word shirt" mis-match by checking the tee set before the shirt set with an explicit fallback.
  - **Gender inferred, not shown:** when the query implies a gender ("mens…", "womens…", "kids…") it's applied as a pre-scope but the redundant Gender facet is hidden (new `Facet.hidden`; `SearchResults` skips hidden facets in the sheet + active-count, and Clear resets to the inferred pre-scope). Gender still shows when the query is gender-neutral ("formal shirt").
  - Verified the resolver across 13 queries (tees/jeans/polo/shirt/shorts/track pants/fabric/lipstick/shoes/bags/saree/jacket/dress) — each yields a focused, relevant facet set; `tsc`/build clean; deployed.

- 2026-07-04: **Search page — functional voice + image search.** The mic and "Photo Search" buttons were dead; both now work.
  - **Voice search (no plugin/key):** new `src/hooks/useSpeechSearch.ts` wraps the browser's built-in Web Speech API (`SpeechRecognition`/`webkitSpeechRecognition`). Tapping the mic starts listening (pulsing mic + "Listening… speak now" hint), live-fills the input with interim transcripts, and runs the search on the final result. Graceful `supported` flag → toast on Firefox/unsupported. Works in Chrome/Edge/Safari over HTTPS after the mic prompt.
  - **Image search:** "Click a photo" (camera `capture="environment"`) and "Select a photo" (gallery) trigger hidden file inputs; the chosen image shows in an "Analyzing image…" overlay while it's sent to a new **`image-search` Supabase Edge Function** (`supabase/functions/image-search/index.ts`, `supabase.functions.invoke`). The function calls a vision model (**OpenAI `gpt-4o-mini`** default, `IMAGE_SEARCH_MODEL` overridable; image sent at `detail:"low"` for speed/cost) to turn the photo into a 3–6-word product query, which the page then runs against the normal catalogue search. CORS-enabled, `verify_jwt` on (anon/user JWT passes). Returns `{error:"not_configured"}` until the key is set, and the client shows a friendly provider-neutral toast for that + other failures.
  - **Requires (image only):** an `OPENAI_API_KEY` secret in Supabase → Edge Functions → Secrets (voice needs nothing). Verified: function deployed + reachable (`not_configured` without key), `tsc`/build clean, deployed to production. (Originally scaffolded on Anthropic Claude, switched to OpenAI at the user's request.)

- 2026-07-04: **Completed the Saved (wishlist) + Recently-Viewed migration to Supabase.** Both `useSyncExternalStore` stores are now **DB-aware without any consumer changes** — the synchronous public API (`useSaved`/`openSaveModal`/`saveToFolders`/`createFolder`/`renameFolder`/`deleteFolder`/`removeFromFolder`/`unsaveProduct`; `useRecentlyViewed`/`recordView`/`removeRecent`/`clearRecent`) is preserved.
  - **Bridge:** new `src/components/StoreSync.tsx` (mounted in `App.tsx`) pushes `useAuth().user?.id` into `setSavedUser()` / `setRecentUser()`. On sign-in the stores **hydrate from the DB** (replacing the local seed) and mirror every mutation fire-and-forget; on sign-out they revert to the localStorage seed. `commit()` skips localStorage while signed in (DB is source of truth).
  - **Saved model:** `saved_items` = the "All Saves" master set; `saved_folders` + `saved_folder_items` = named folders. `createFolder` generates a client-side UUID when signed in and inserts it explicitly so local ids === DB ids (the modal's create-then-select flow keeps working). Hydration rebuilds rich `SavedProduct`s by joining `products` (new shared `fetchProductCardsByIds` in `queries/products.ts`). Recently-Viewed hydrates `recently_viewed` the same way. Only real product UUIDs are written (FK-guarded); synthetic/demo ids stay local-only.
  - **Verified:** `tsc` + build clean; RLS round-trip with a real demo-buyer JWT — own insert/read 201/200, **spoofing another buyer's `buyer_id` blocked (403)**, anon read `[]`; deployed, `/saved` + `/recently-viewed` return 200. A new signed-in user now starts with a real (empty) wishlist/history instead of the seeded demo items.

Now **26 live products / 6 vendors / 11 categories**.

Also fixed the cards' placeholder "Product name" to render the real product name.

- 2026-07-04: **Added Gujarati (ગુજરાતી) as a third language.** Generalized the i18n store from en/hi to multi-language: `Lang` is now `"en" | "hi" | "gu"`, `LANG_OPTIONS`/`SUPPORTED`/`read()`/`setLang()` updated, and `langCodeFromName()` maps Gujarati/ગુજરાતી → `gu` (checked before Hindi). Added a full **`GU` dictionary** in `i18n.ts` mirroring every `HI` key (~180 entries), collected into `DICTS = { hi, gu }`; `translate()`/`lookup()` now take the language and read from `DICTS[lang]`. Updated `AutoTranslate.tsx` to translate for any non-English language and added a `LAST_SET` WeakMap so **direct hi↔gu switches** translate from each node's stored English rather than from the other language's already-rendered text (previously the restore/translate logic assumed only hi↔en). Added the **ગુજરાતી** button to the vendor MyStore selector; the buyer Login modal and Profile → Regional Settings already listed Gujarati and now resolve it via `langCodeFromName`. Verified in Playwright: GU `/profile` shows 30 Gujarati body strings, English fully restores (0 Devanagari/0 Gujarati), and a **live en→hi→gu→en** switch via Regional Settings flips the nav through श्रेणियाँ → શ્રેણીઓ → Categories with no reload and no errors. Build clean.

- 2026-07-04: **Language now changes EVERYWHERE — global DOM auto-translator.** The per-component `useT()` wrapping only translated strings each page explicitly opted in, so switching to Hindi changed the nav but left most page bodies in English ("not changing from everywhere"). Added `src/components/i18n/AutoTranslate.tsx` — mounted once at the app root inside `<BrowserRouter>` — which walks the rendered DOM and translates any text node / `placeholder` whose English matches the dictionary, with **no per-component wrapping**. It: re-applies on language change and on route/content changes via a `MutationObserver` (rAF-batched), stores each translated node's original English in a `WeakMap` to restore on switch-back, is idempotent (React re-renders that reset text get re-translated), skips `script/style/textarea/code/pre` and `[data-no-translate]`, and leaves dictionary-missing strings as English. Also **greatly expanded** the `HI` dictionary in `i18n.ts` (~180 entries: common actions/buttons, auth, commerce, RFQ/quotes, profile, chat/support, vendor dashboard, sort/filter, section headings) and exported `lookupHi()`. Net effect: one language switch now translates page bodies across the whole app (buyer + vendor), not just chrome. Verified in Playwright: EN pages have 0 Hindi nodes; HI `/profile` shows 26 translated body strings (गतिविधि, खाता सेटिंग्स, मेरी समीक्षाएँ, व्यवसाय विवरण…), HI `/search/results` translates Brand Picks/sponsored/Call Now/Sort/Filter; switching back to English fully restores; no console/page errors. Build clean.

- 2026-07-04: **Functional language translation (English / Hindi) — vendor + buyer.** The three previously-disconnected, non-functional language selectors now drive one shared global switch that actually translates the UI. New `src/lib/i18n.ts` — a module-level store (`useSyncExternalStore` + `localStorage` key `cosora.lang`, sets `document.documentElement.lang`) exposing `useLang()`, `setLang()`, `langCodeFromName()`, and `useT()`. `useT()` returns a translate fn keyed by the **English string** (`t("New Arrivals")`), so components wrap existing text with zero key bookkeeping and any unlisted string safely falls back to English. Scope = app **chrome** (nav, buttons, headings, labels); dynamic data (product names, vendor descriptions, chat/user text) intentionally left as entered — chosen approach per the user (global switch + curated dictionary, EN+HI to start; extensible). **Selectors wired:** buyer Login `LanguageModal`, buyer Profile → Regional Settings `<select>`, and vendor MyStore English/हिंदी buttons all call `setLang()` (Login/regional map any language name → en/hi; only those two are translated today, others fall back to English). **Chrome translated:** buyer `MobileBottomNav` + seller nav, `DashboardSidebar` (buyer & seller menus + Switch Mode/Support labels), `BuyerHomeTabs`, the inline home-feed tab strip on NewArrivals/Trends/Sale/ForYou/Following, `BuyerTopBar` (drawer menu, Buyer/Seller switch, search placeholder, currency/support), `SubmitRequirementCard`, and SearchResults (FILTER/SORT, Call Now, filter-sheet Filters/Refining/Clear/Show). Verified in Playwright: buyer mobile nav + feed tabs render Hindi with `docLang=hi`, English default unaffected, vendor sidebar renders Hindi, and a **live switch** via Regional Settings updates the nav instantly + persists to `localStorage`. Build clean.

- 2026-07-04: **Context-aware Search Results filters.** The filter panel now adapts to *what* the buyer searched — different searches surface different, relevant facets. New `src/lib/searchFilters.ts` defines ~16 product domains mirroring the category taxonomy (what Cosora sells) with a `resolveFilterSchema(query)` resolver: e.g. **"mens shorts"** → *Men's Shorts* (Category-within-Menswear, Gender, Fit, Fabric, Waist Size, Length, Pattern, Colour, GSM, Price, MOQ, Rating, Vendor) with **gender pre-scoped to Men**; **"fabric"** → Fabric Type / GSM / Width / Construction / Composition; **"lipstick"** → Product Type / Skin Type / Formulation / Concern / Finish; plus footwear (Sole, UK Size), bags, packaging (Material, Printing), trims, home textile, ethnic (Work/Embellishment, Occasion), dresses, winterwear, innerwear. Each schema prepends a **Category facet built from `CATEGORY_TAXONOMY`** (the searched category's sibling subcategories). Domain matching is whole-word + plural-tolerant so "bag" doesn't fire inside "poly**bag**" (Packaging ordered before Bags for "paper bag"). Rebuilt `SearchResults.tsx`: replaced the static GENDER/SORT/FILTER dropdowns with **SORT + a context-aware FILTER bottom-sheet** ("Refining {domain}", chip facets, live "Show N results", Clear-all, active-count badge on the bar). Filtering engine (`matchFacet`) maps facets to real product fields (gender/fabric/fit/price-bucket/gsm-bucket/rating/verified) and derives a stable pseudo-value (`hashPick`) for facets without backing data, so every chip narrows deterministically. framer-motion sheet, safe-area footer, `useReducedMotion`-gated. Verified in Playwright across 8 queries (correct domain + facets each, gender pre-scope, live count changes, **no dup facets, no console errors**); build clean.

- 2026-07-03: **Mobile "whole site zoomed out into one frame" fix + smaller buyer logo.** (1) `BuyerTopBar` header (`logo → search(flex-1, max-w-md) → bookmark → 3-dot`) overflowed the viewport by **49px** on phones: the search button had no `min-w-0`, so its flex `min-width:auto` equalled the full no-wrap placeholder width and refused to shrink, pushing the icons past the right edge → mobile browsers scaled the whole page down to fit. Added `min-w-0` to the search button (text already truncates). Surfaced after the wordmark became an image (wider than the old text). Verified 0px horizontal overflow at 390px across landing + all buyer pages (home/for-you/categories/profile/saved/chats/search/browse). - 2026-07-03: **OTP page — Previous/Next stay above the number pad.** On `OtpVerify.tsx` the Previous/Next bar sat in normal flow at the bottom of a `min-h-screen` container, so the Android number pad covered it. Added a `useKeyboardInset()` hook (VisualViewport API: `innerHeight − visualViewport.height − offsetTop`) and made the action bar `fixed bottom-0`, translating it up by the keyboard height so it stays visible/tappable while entering the OTP (returns to the bottom when the keyboard closes). Page-scoped (no global viewport-meta change), safe-area-inset aware, smooth transition. Verified with a mocked VisualViewport in Playwright: bar is `position:fixed`, lifts `translateY(-320px)` for a 320px keyboard, and resets to `none` on close — no console errors; build clean.

- 2026-07-02: **Garment Configurator (Design Studio)** at `/configurator` — buyer custom-apparel designer inspired by virtualthreads.
  - **v2 (current) — realistic photo-mockup approach.** The initial three.js version looked game-y (procedural meshes) and virtualthreads is actually 2D photographic compositing, so it was rebuilt: **removed three.js/@react-three deps + deleted `GarmentScene.tsx`**; reverted the App lazy-load. Now `src/lib/configuratorData.ts` carries a `MOCKUPS` map (real Unsplash garment photos front/back per garment + a `print` zone as % of the image). `src/pages/Configurator.tsx` renders `GarmentMockup`: real garment photo (`object-cover`, with an `onError` fallback), **recolor via a `mix-blend-mode: color` tint** (preserves real fabric shading; white = natural photo, skipped tint), and the **design composited into the print zone with `mix-blend-mode: multiply`** so it reads as ink on fabric (light text auto-switches to `normal` blend so it stays visible). The garment itself is the editable surface — drag text/artwork directly on it. Tools: garment picker (10 types across tops/fleece/bottoms), Add Text (font/color/scale/rotation), Upload Artwork (PNG/JPG/SVG), color swatches, fabric+GSM, print method, sizes+quantity. Finish → **Save & Request Quote** (localStorage + route to `/requirement/post-requirement`). Entry points: BuyerTopBar drawer "Design Studio" + "Design a Custom Garment (3D)" card in the Requirement hub. Bundle back to ~2.2 MB (no WebGL, works on all devices). Tradeoff: the recolor tints the whole frame (no per-image garment mask) and print-zone rects are generic per archetype — swap in transparent-PNG mockups + per-image zones for pixel-perfect placement later.
  - **Polish pass:** garment-image **crossfade** on change (AnimatePresence), **smooth recolor** transition (softened to 0.92), studio radial backdrop + vignette, rounded-3xl preview, loading pulse, section **stagger-in** + micro-interactions (whileTap/hover), animated **layoutId front/back toggle**, **grouped garment picker** (Tops/Fleece/Bottoms), crisp non-blended **selection outline + in-canvas delete (X) button**, animated selected-layer panel, empty-zone hint. All `useReducedMotion`-gated.

Now uses **BuyerShell** (was the vendor `DashboardLayout`) + coral `#ef4d62` (was theme tokens). New **center-peek swipeable media carousel** (`MediaCarousel`): neighbours peek, drag/dots, **tiled COSORA watermark**, **video autoplay** when a video slide is active (`<video autoplay muted loop>`, sample mp4 added to media), rating badge, **TrustedSEAL image only when `isInAd`**. Sections: breadcrumb, name + bookmark(→`openSaveModal`) + share(copies link), ₹price/unit, MOQ + product code, **clickable vendor card** (→`/vendor/:id`, Follow toggle, Trust Seal Verified when ad). Details/Reviews **animated tabs** (`layoutId`). Details: colors, sizes, **inline Chat (filled) + Call Now (outline)**, **collapsible Product Specifications** (+ Add Fabric, Download PDF), Customization YES/NO, Description (+Translate), Manufacturing (Made in + certs + View Profile). Reviews: 4.x score + animated breakdown bars + review list (Helpful vote, size bought, photos) + **Write a Review modal**. **Brand Picks** sponsored strip + **You might also like** using the shared `ListingProductCard`. Records view via `recordView`. Note (not built): a Service/Freelancer product variant should mirror the vendor upload fields — deferred.

- 2026-07-02: **Buyer Search — interactivity refinements** — (1) The "we recommend" name is now the **real user's first name** from `profileStore` (`useProfileState().profile.fullName`), not hardcoded. (2) **Trending Keywords are now selectable filters**: each chip maps to a `TrendCat`; tapping one **darkens it** (active = solid `bg-gray-900`, hides its thumbnail) and **filters the recommendation feed** below to that category (first chip active by default; tap again to clear → all). Recommendation pool re-tagged with categories (~3 per trend); feed resets to batch 1 on filter change. (3) **Popular keywords slider is now swipeable** (framer-motion `drag="x"` + `onDragEnd` page change, grab cursor) in addition to the dot indicators.

Typing state: animated scrim + dropdown with **top-5 product/category suggestions (result counts + match highlight)** then **5 brand stores (logo + verified + followers)** → brand taps route to `/vendor/:id`. Added framer-motion throughout (page/section stagger, dropdown fade/slide, keyword-page swap, tap feedback) gated by `useReducedMotion`. Coral mic accent.

Routed from every buyer surface that links `/vendor/:id` (product cards, ProductDetail vendor card, Trends, Following, Search) and from the Quotes **View Profile** button (`QuoteDetailsModal` now navigates to `/vendor/:vendorId`). Follow state is local (not yet persisted to `followingStore`); banner/product imagery uses existing placeholder assets.

- 2026-07-01: **Buyer "My Previous Quotes" page** — Built the buyer quotes-management flow at `/requirement/my-quotes` (previously mis-pointed at the vendor `<Quotes />` page — now `<MyQuotes />`). New data/store: `src/lib/quotesData.ts` (RFQs + received `VendorQuote`s, `fmtMoney`, per-vendor `currency` + INR-normalized `priceINR`) and `src/lib/quotesStore.ts` (`useSyncExternalStore` + localStorage; `setQuoteStatus`, `quotesForRfq`, `rfqStats`, `bestPrice`/`topRated` ranked on `priceINR`). Page `src/pages/MyQuotes.tsx` has two internal views: **My Quotes** (stats: Active RFQs / Total / New; request cards with status + "N new" badge, image on right) and **Quotes Received** (2×2 stats, Best Price + Top Rated cards, search + status filter + sort, per-quote cards). Components under `src/components/buyer/quotes/`: `ReceivedQuoteCard` (status-driven Reject/Shortlist/Accept actions), `QuoteDetailsModal` (vendor profile, attachments images/videos/docs, vendor-quote grid incl. **Fabric** + Sample Timeline, Payment Terms input, Vendor Notes, chat-with-vendor), `VendorChatModal` (pop-up chat, buyer coral bubbles), `CompareTable` (up to 3 quotes; rows Price/MOQ/Lead/Fabric/Rating/Actions). Accept→green banner, Reject→red banner. Removed 3 superseded scaffold routes (`.../quotes-received`, `.../quote-detail/:quoteId`, `.../compare`) from `buyerShellRoutes` in `App.tsx`. Currency note honored: Indian vendors show ₹, foreign vendors (e.g. Bangladesh) show their currency; ranking uses `priceINR` so cross-currency Best Price is correct (₹245 Textile Masters).

- 2026-07-01: **Buyer-side build-out (major)** — Built/rewrote the buyer experience. New reactive state pattern used throughout: module-level stores backed by `useSyncExternalStore` + `localStorage` (no React context), so bookmarks/follows/prefs/views stay in sync across every page. Buyer pages use `BuyerShell` (`src/components/buyer/BuyerShell.tsx` — BuyerTopBar + content + MobileBottomNav + ToTop), NOT the vendor `DashboardLayout`.
  - **Product Detail (buyer)** — `src/pages/ProductDetail.tsx` rewritten: media carousel w/ COSORA watermark, vendor card, Details/Reviews tabs, specs grid, sticky Chat/Call bar (`bottom-[73px]` on mobile to clear MobileBottomNav). Records views via `recordView()` on mount.
  - **Search** — `src/pages/Search.tsx` (route `/search`): default state (photo search, recent/popular/trending) + live typing overlay (product/category/brand matches).
  - **Trends** — `src/pages/Trends.tsx` (route `/home/trends`): trend insights, category chips, featured image, Top Brands, Hot Trends/Keywords, infinite feed. Uses `BuyerTopBar` + `MobileBottomNav autoHide`.
  - **Following** — `src/pages/Following.tsx` + `FollowingViewAll.tsx` (routes `/home/followings`, `/home/followings/view-all`) backed by `src/lib/followingStore.ts` (follow/unfollow/hide brands).
  - **For You** — `src/pages/ForYou.tsx` (route `/home/for-you`): 3-step onboarding + preference-filtered feed, backed by `src/lib/preferencesStore.ts` (categories/locations/hasCompleted).
  - **My Profile** — `src/pages/Profile.tsx` rewritten + `ProfileNotifications.tsx`, `ProfileSocialLinks.tsx`, `ProfileAccountPrefs.tsx`, `TermsConditions.tsx` (routes `/profile/notifications`, `/profile/social-links`, `/profile/regional-settings`, `/profile/terms`) backed by `src/lib/profileStore.ts`. Regional settings kept INR/IST per constraint (overrode mockup USD/ET). Terms jurisdiction filled "Mumbai, India".
  - **Wishlist system** — `src/lib/savedStore.ts` (folders + products + pending-modal, seeded), `src/components/buyer/SaveToFolderModal.tsx` (global modal mounted once in `App.tsx` inside `<BrowserRouter>`), `src/pages/SavedCollections.tsx` + `SavedCollectionDetail.tsx` (routes `/saved`, `/saved/:collectionId`). Any card bookmark calls `openSaveModal(product)`. Wired across `BuyerProductCard.tsx`, `ListingProductCard.tsx` (+ `src/lib/listingProducts.ts` types/helpers), `NewArrivals.tsx`, `Trends.tsx`, `Search.tsx`, and `EverydayFashionHero.tsx` (the last two previously used dead local state — fixed).

Field types: text/textarea/description(voice)/select/chips/radio/priceRange/files. Real Web Speech API voice-to-text on Description.
  - **Recently Viewed** — `src/pages/RecentlyViewed.tsx` rewritten (route `/recently-viewed`) backed by new `src/lib/recentlyViewedStore.ts` (`recordView`/`removeRecent`/`clearRecent`/`useRecentlyViewed`/`relativeTime`, seeded, localStorage). Header trash → "Clear viewing history?" confirm dialog; per-card trash deletes instantly; GENDER/SORT/FILTER bar; heart routes through `openSaveModal`.
  - **MobileBottomNav** — `src/components/layout/MobileBottomNav.tsx` gained optional `autoHide?: boolean` (scroll-direction hide/show).

- 2026-06-17: **Full vendor side animation pass** — Applied consistent Framer Motion animation system across all 26 vendor-side pages. Animation constants used everywhere:
  ```tsx
  const E = [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1];   // strong ease-out cubic-bezier
  const page = { hidden: {}, show: { transition: { staggerChildren: 0.07, delayChildren: 0.04 } } };
  const section = { hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 18 }, show: { opacity: 1, y: 0, transition: { ease: E, duration: 0.38 } } };
  const listContainer = { show: { transition: { staggerChildren: 0.055 } } };
  const listItem = { hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 10 }, show: { opacity: 1, y: 0, transition: { ease: E, duration: 0.26 } } };
  ```
  Rules: `useReducedMotion()` in every page, `whileTap={{ scale: 0.97 }}` on every button, `whileHover={{ x: 3 }}` on ArrowRight icons, no `transition: all`. Pages animated: `Index.tsx`, `Products.tsx`, `Leads.tsx`, `Quotes.tsx`, `Upload.tsx`, `Advertisements.tsx`, `AdvertisementSlideshow.tsx`, `OldAdvertisements.tsx`, `CompetitorAds.tsx`, `BusinessProfile.tsx`, `BusinessProfileEmployees.tsx`, `MyStore.tsx`, `MyBusiness.tsx`, `BusinessTools.tsx`, `BusinessProfileScorePage.tsx`, `Analytics.tsx`, `Reviews.tsx`, `Subscription.tsx`, `UploadCatalogue.tsx`, `UploadVideo.tsx`, `Help.tsx`, `ReportFraud.tsx`, `AppFeedback.tsx`, `VendorBlogs.tsx`, `VendorBlogArticle.tsx`.

Changes: added missing `DashboardLayout` wrapper (CLAUDE.md constraint), removed fixed `maxWidth: 390px` → `max-w-xl mx-auto`, removed Google Fonts `@import`, converted all inline styles to Tailwind, each social platform gets its own card (`bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 shadow-sm`), added `description` field to `SocialPlatform` interface, `PlatformCard` component with `useReducedMotion()` internally, completion `CheckCircle2` springs in when any URL filled, field add/remove animates with height 0→auto + opacity, save button morphs between "Save Social Links" and "Saved ✓" via `AnimatePresence mode="wait"`, header shows live progress (`X of N platforms connected`).

- 2026-06-17: **`src/pages/SellerHome.tsx` animation rewrite** — Applied Emil Kowalski philosophy via `emil-design-eng` skill. Page wrapper uses `variants={page}` stagger, all 11 sections use `variants={section}`, stat cards / lead cards / messages use `listContainer`/`listItem` stagger, carousel `transition-all` replaced with explicit cubic-bezier opacity+transform, `whileTap` on every button, `whileHover={{ x: 3 }}` on ArrowRight, competitor card gets `whileHover={{ scale: 1.012 }}`, `useReducedMotion()` disables all animation for accessibility.
